Worth checking because InferAttributes is a TWO-POINT fit (f[0] and last nonzero only) that cannot see table interiors - its classification is now evidence, not assumption.
BLOCKING DEFECT FOUND: rewrite_rope.cc:351 emits both position adapters as BROADCAST_TO, the one broadcast-shaped op ML Drift does NOT implement - support.cc has no kTfLiteBuiltinBroadcastTo case and ends default:return false, and identifier BroadcastTo occurs nowhere in ml_drift_delegate/ (the BROADCAST_TO string in the accelerator is the builtin enum NAME TABLE, not evidence of support). Both adapters consume runtime decode_input_pos:0 and feed all 50 composites, so they fall back to CPU and the decode graph can no longer report one LITERT_CL partition - the invariant every GPU run here gates on. Absolute per-token cost is small (36 B INT32, two trivial ops) and UNMEASURED; the risk is invalidating the A/B's own placement gate and misreading it as "fusion did not help". FIX: decode_input_pos:0 is rank-1 [1] and TILE cannot change rank, so RESHAPE([1]->[1,1,1,1]) alone replaces the width-1 adapter and RESHAPE+TILE(multiples [1,1,8,1]) the width-8 one; both supported, node count unchanged, reduction stays 400. Do NOT use STABLEHLO_BROADCAST_IN_DIM: pinned schema declares broadcast_dimensions as a BUILTIN OPTION while IsBroadcastInDimSupported reads a SECOND INPUT TENSOR; encoding unsettled.
Remaining gates before any device A/B: apply adapter fix + re-run verifier; sweep all emitted opcodes (incl. decomposition subgraphs) against support.cc's accepted set; repack section 10 (+352,192 B, section 11 at 2543812608, 16 KiB alignment -> must shift, in-place overwrite unsafe); then A/B vs the 12.4527 tok/s exact same-queue record gating on one LITERT_CL partition + output SHA 2ec35bec... + thermal 0. CAVEAT: composites compute sin/cos on the fly vs the original precomputed table - agreement is float32-rounding-level, so byte-identical output is a HYPOTHESIS to test, not a guarantee. Durable: rope-freq-verify/RESULTS.md + verify_rope_freq.cc + dump_pos.cc. Astra's files untouched.
